866 entries, feature-diagrams to feature-hashing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **feature-diagrams**: Draws the two code-derived diagrams of a finished feature, how it is built (architecture) and what the user does (workflow), as validated Archify HTML. Covers 7 phases: Brief, Architecture, Plan, Implement, Review, Push, Cleanup. Enforces TDD, structured code review, success metrics, and human review gates between phases. Do not use for quick fixes, single-file changes, or trivial features that touch fewer than 3 files.
